# How this repository was made (Lazio)

This file is written by the build on 2026-10-30, the day the source data was retrieved.

## Sources

| Source | Address | Retrieved | Licence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Consiglio regionale del Lazio, Leggi regionali (testo coordinato) | <https://www.consiglio.regione.lazio.it/?vw=leggiregionali> | 2026-10-30 | L. 633/1941 art. 5: official acts are not protected. Site footer: "Copyright 2016 - Consiglio regionale del Lazio"; images under CC BY-NC-SA 2.0. No permission requirement for the texts. |

Every word of law in this repository comes from these sources. Lexifina reads them and records each act's dated versions, and the laws that made each change, in one corpus file. This repository is built from that file.

The build made 1908 commits:

| Commits | Kind |
| ---: | --- |
| 1908 | first version of an act |
| 0 | one law on its in-force day |

Other counts:
- 0 commits on the branch `futuro`: laws not yet in force on 2026-10-30.
- 0 commits dated 1 January 1970: in force before 1970, with the real day in their `In-force` trailer.
- 1908 laws in the record.
- 1908 tags.

## Dates

Each commit is dated at noon (Europe/Rome) on the day the change took effect. The other dates, enacted and published, are trailers.

When a law gives no date of its own, the default rule applies: Statuto della Regione Lazio, art. 39: a regional law enters into force on the fifteenth day after its publication in the Bollettino ufficiale, unless it sets another term. (Not applied here: every text is the current one, dated the retrieval day.) https://www.consiglio.regione.lazio.it/?vw=leggiregionali

## How the history is built

`tools/build.py` turns the corpus into this history in one pass: one commit per law, on the day it took effect. The same corpus always gives the same commit hashes. `tools/check_repo.py` reads every version back from git and compares it with the corpus. `tools/on.py` shows the law as it stood on any date. The corpus format is described in `tools/SCHEMA.md`.

- Corpus: sha256 `e655be20cb40287ec300535020727350c9194cd1fba7bc8609c98492bed5d4ad` (31537270 bytes).
- Builder blob hashes (`git hash-object`): `build.py` `a4faa25af85facb64b956b372f6b528aafdd7ec1`, `check.py` `3d00541d258745461ab52b1b0cb4e1716a27a95d`, `on.py` `644a6c0857fbcc72562032b58d57aa113fee0641`.
- Python 3.12.10; git version 2.46.0.windows.1.

## Checks

1908 versions of 1908 acts read back from git on the day each took effect and on the day before: 0 mismatches. Trailers: 0 commits whose trailers git cannot parse. Health: 0 merge commits on main, 0 commit dates that go backwards, 0 commits dated after the build.

No second build of this corpus has been compared yet.

## Decisions made by hand

- Current-only: each act is the coordinated text the Consiglio regionale del Lazio publishes on the retrieval day, as one "oldest version available" commit dated that day, naming the act itself as the earlier law.
- Units: intestazione (everything the page prints before the first article heading, including its summary), one file per article (from its "Art. N" heading), and note (the editor's notes printed after the text). Tables are one cell per line. The closing line "Il testo non ha valore legale ..." is left out.
- Laws whose page opens by saying the whole law is repealed ("legge abrogata") are left out.
- Statute: the Consiglio's consolidated PDF (October 2013), its text lines joined into paragraphs; page numbers are left out, footnotes stay where the PDF prints them.

## What AI did and did not do

The scripts that read the sources and build this repository were written by Claude Code running Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) under Lexifina's direction.

No model wrote, translated or corrected legal text. No model runs at build time. Every word of law in this repository comes from the official sources above.

The commit messages are written by `build.py` from the source data.

Model use at data-build time:
- A model (Claude) wrote the fetch and parse code and chose the layout; it wrote no law text. Every word of law comes from the Consiglio regionale del Lazio's pages and PDF.
